# Post Control System (PCS) ↔ Elchi — To‘liq Funksional Taqqoslash

> Avtomatik ko‘p-agentli tahlil (12 domen, 994 funksiya). PCS = `post_control_system` (front+back bitta repo, NestJS monolit). Elchi = `Elchi-Frontend` (React FSD) + `Elchi-Backend` (NestJS, 14 mikroservis, api-gateway).

**Belgilar:** ✅ = bor · ❌ = yo‘q · 🟡 = qisman/cheklangan

## Umumiy ko‘rinish (domenlar bo‘yicha)

|---|-------|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
| 1 | Buyurtmalar (Orders) | 74 | 48 | 9 | 17 |
| 2 | Skanerlash (Scan / QR) | 54 | 19 | 8 | 27 |
| 3 | Yangi buyurtmalar / Market tasdiqlash (New Orders) | 60 | 30 | 12 | 18 |
| 4 | Integratsiyalar / Tashqi buyurtmalar (Integrations / External) | 114 | 38 | 57 | 19 |
| 5 | Pochta / Partiya / Qaytarishlar (Mails / Post / Returns) | 97 | 45 | 30 | 22 |
| 6 | Mahsulotlar (Products / Catalog) | 83 | 48 | 17 | 18 |
| 7 | Foydalanuvchilar / Rollar / Identity / Profil / Auth (Users) | 84 | 51 | 18 | 15 |
| 8 | To’lovlar / Kassa / Balans / Hisob-kitob (Payments / Cashbox / Finance) | 83 | 41 | 26 | 16 |
| 9 | Hududlar / Tumanlar / SATO / Logist / Dispatch (Regions / Logistics) | 84 | 47 | 32 | 5 |

| 11 | Filiallar / Partiyalar / Operatorlar / Investorlar / C2C (Branch system) | 89 | 12 | 12 | 65 |

| | **JAMI** | **994** | **442** | **260** | **292** |

## 1. Buyurtmalar (Orders)

Orders ikkala loyihada ham eng yirik domen. PCS — monolit (server/src/api/order/order.controller.ts + order.service.ts ~6000 qator); REST endpointlar to'g'ridan-to'g'ri rol guard bilan himoyalangan. Elchi — mikroservis: REST yuzi apps/api-gateway/src/order-gateway.controller.ts (~2280 qator) → RabbitMQ {cmd:'order.*'} message-patternlari order-service ga, kuryer biriktirish esa logistics-service ga uzatiladi. Funksional yadro (yaratish oqimi market→mijoz→mahsulot→tasdiq, ro'yxat filtr/qidiruv/status, kuryer sell/cancel/partly-sell/rollback, bulk, excel export) ikkalasida ham bor. Asosiy farq: PCS kuryerga biriktirishni alohida "post" (jo'natma) domeni orqali qiladi, Elchi esa order-level assign-to-courier/scan-assign hamda branch/settlement/return/could-not-deliver oqimlarini orderga integratsiya qilgan; aksincha PCS-da tashqi integratsiya, telegram bot va tarif tahrirlash UI'si yetukroq.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Orders (asosiy ro'yxat) — admin/superadmin/registrator/logist (AdminSidebar/RegistratorSidebar/LogistSidebar: /orders → Filter + OrderView jadval)
- Orders (market/operator ko'rinishi) — market/operator uchun shu /orders sahifa o'z buyurtmalari rejimida (getMarketsAllNewOrder)
- Choose-market → Customer-info → Confirm (create-order) — buyurtma yaratish 3-bosqichli oqimi (admin/superadmin/registrator/market/operator)
- Order-detail/:id (order-details) — buyurtma detali: itemlar, QR, meta, mijoz, manzil, kuryer, tracking timeline, rollback/tarif/o'chirish (admin/superadmin/market/operator rollarga ko'ra)
- Courier-orders → Kutilayotgan / Hammasi / Bekor qilingan tablari (courier) — sell/cancel/partly-sell/rollback + bekor qilinganlarni pochtaga yuborish
- Scan/:token (ScanAndOrder) — kuryer QR skan qilib sell/cancel/partly/rollback/receive (courier)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Orders (asosiy ro'yxat) — admin/superadmin/registrator/manager/market (pages/orders/index.tsx: OrderFilters + OrdersTable)
- Manager tablari: Pending / All / Cancelled (OrderTabs) — faqat HYBRID/REGIONAL filial managerlari uchun (sell/cancel/rollback table action + cancelledlarni HQ ga yuborish)
- Add (create) — OrderStepper: Step1 Market → Step2 Customer → Step3 Details (mahsulot+narx+yetkazish) (pages/orders/create)
- edit/:orderId va :marketId/edit/:orderId (NewOrderUpdate) — buyurtma tahrirlash + OrderTracking widget (new_orders domeni komponentidan)
- Courier orders (CourierOrders) → Pending / All / Cancelled tablari — sell/cancel/partly-sell/rollback modallari (proof upload bilan) + cancelledlarni pochtaga yuborish
- Dispatch (pages/dispatch) — manager/branch buyurtmalarni kuryerga bulk biriktirish (assign-to-courier)

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Buyurtma yaratish — to'liq oqim (market→mijoz→mahsulot→tasdiqlash) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /order (createOrder) \| Elchi: POST /orders → order.create (gateway avval identity.customer.create chaqiradi) — PCS: choose-market→customer-info→confirm sahifalari, localStorage orqali. Elchi: bitta sahifadagi 3-bosqichli OrderStepper (react-hook-form). |
| 2 | Mijoz tanlash/yaratish (yangi yoki mavjud) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user (createUser customer) alohida + order customer_id \| Elchi: gateway customer obyektini order.create ichida identity.customer.create bilan yaratadi — PCS customer-info sahifasi mijoz tarixini ham ko'rsatadi (getMarketNewOrders). |
| 3 | Mahsulot qo'shish + miqdor (+/-) + narx + yetkazish turi (markaz/uy) + izoh + operator | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: order.items + total_price + where_deliver + comment + operator \| Elchi: dto.items + total_price + where_deliver + comment + operator (Step3Details) — PCS create-order operator telefon raqamini ham yuboradi. |
| 4 | Telegram bot orqali buyurtma yaratish | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /order/telegram/bot/create (botOrderCreate) \| Elchi: POST /orders/telegram/bot/create → mappingdan keyin order.create — Ikkalasida ham operator/market roli; Elchi MARKET_OPERATOR market_id'ni server tomonda resolve qiladi. |
| 5 | Buyurtmalar ro'yxati (jadval) — pagination, ustunlar (mijoz, telefon, tuman, market, status, narx, sana) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order (findAll) \| Elchi: GET /orders → order.find_all_enriched (fallback find_all) — Ikkalasida desktop jadval + mobil karta. Elchi limit faqat 10/25/50/100; pagination meta normalizatsiyasi gatewayda. |
| 6 | Filtr: market bo'yicha | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order?marketId \| Elchi: GET /orders?market_id — Market/operator roli o'zinikidan boshqasini ko'ra olmaydi (ikkala tomonda guard). |
| 7 | Filtr: viloyat (region) bo'yicha | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: ?regionId \| Elchi: ?region_id — Elchi manager roli uchun region o'rniga district filtri ko'rsatiladi. |
| 8 | Filtr: tuman (district) bo'yicha | 🟡 | ✅ | PCS: kuryer tabida ?district_id bor (getCourierOrders) \| Elchi: GET /orders?district_id — PCS asosiy admin filtrida district yo'q, faqat kuryer tabida; Elchi managerga district selecti. |
| 9 | Filtr: kuryer bo'yicha | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: ?courierId (client-side region bilan filter) \| Elchi: ?courier_id — Region tanlanganda kuryer tanlovi ikkalasida ham tozalanadi. |
| 10 | Filtr: filial (branch) bo'yicha | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: GET /orders?branch_id (faqat admin/superadmin), branch staff uchun gateway o'z branchiga majburlaydi — PCS-da filial tizimi yo'q; Elchi-da branch scoping butun orders qatlamida. |
| 11 | Filtr: status (ko'p tanlovli multi-select) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: ?status (massiv) \| Elchi: ?status (vergul/massiv, gateway parseStatusQuery bilan validatsiya) — Elchi noto'g'ri status qiymatida 400 qaytaradi. |
| 12 | Filtr: sana oralig'i (start/end date range) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: ?startDate&endDate \| Elchi: ?start_day&end_day — PCS antd RangePicker; Elchi FilterDateRange. Elchi'da aktiv filtrlar 'chip' ko'rinishida ham ko'rsatiladi. |
| 13 | Qidiruv (mijoz ism/familiya/telefon) — debounce | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: ?search \| Elchi: ?search — PCS 500ms debounce; Elchi searchSlice orqali alohida. |
| 14 | Aktiv filtrlarni 'chip' sifatida ko'rsatish va alohida o'chirish | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: frontend (FilterChip) — backend yo'q — PCS faqat aktiv filtr sonini badge qiladi; alohida chip yo'q. |
| 15 | Barcha filtrlarni tozalash (Clear) | ✅ | ✅ | frontend (resetFilter/resetFilters) — Ikkalasida ham mavjud. |
| 16 | Excel export (filtrlangan buyurtmalar) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/export/excel (streaming, backend) yoki front fetchAll \| Elchi: frontend paginatsiyalab yig'adi (exportOrdersToExcel), maxsus export endpoint yo'q — PCS-da backend streaming endpoint bor; Elchi mijoz tomonda 100tadan sahifalab yig'adi. |
| 17 | Buyurtma yaratish tugmasi (Add order) + market tanlash modal | ✅ | ✅ | frontend (navigate) \| Elchi PopupSelect market \| PCS navigate choose-market — PCS market roli uchun add_order ruxsatini profildan tekshiradi (togglePermission). |
| 18 | Buyurtma detali sahifasi (itemlar, narx, to'lanishi kerak, izoh, meta) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/:id (findOne) \| Elchi: GET /orders/:id → order.find_by_id_enriched + IDOR himoya (assertCanViewOrder) — Elchi gateway sequential id IDOR'ga qarshi obyekt-darajali avtorizatsiya qo'shgan. |
| 19 | Buyurtmani tahrirlash (to'liq: itemlar/narx/manzil/yetkazish) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: PATCH /order/:id (editOrder) \| Elchi: PATCH /orders/:id va /orders/:id/full → order.update_normalized (SUPERADMIN/ADMIN/REGISTRATOR) — Elchi'da tahrirlash UI'si new_orders domeni (NewOrderUpdate) komponentidan keladi. |
| 20 | Buyurtma yetkazish manzilini alohida yangilash | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS: PATCH /order/:id/address (updateOrderAddress) \| Elchi: order.update_normalized ichida manzil maydonlari (alohida /address endpoint yo'q) — PCS-da ShippingAddress komponenti orqali alohida amal; Elchi'da umumiy update ichida. |
| 21 | Mijoz ism/telefon/manzilini buyurtma detalidan tahrirlash | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: PATCH /user/customer/name-phone/:id, PATCH /user/customer/address/:id \| Elchi: order detalidan customer-edit alohida yo'q — PCS order-details sahifasida CustomerDetail/ShippingAddress canEdit bilan. |
| 22 | Buyurtmani o'chirish (delete) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: DELETE /order/:id (remove) \| Elchi: DELETE /orders/:id → order.delete — PCS: market faqat 'new' statusda; Elchi: status-asosidagi rol qoidalari. |
| 23 | Kuryer: kutilayotgan buyurtmalar tabi (waiting) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/courier/orders?status=waiting \| Elchi: GET /orders/courier/orders?status=waiting — PCS legacy courier endpoint logistics post_ids orqali; Elchi ham courier postlari orqali. |
| 24 | Kuryer: hamma buyurtmalar tabi (all) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS & Elchi: GET .../courier/orders (statussiz) — PCS all-orders tabida sold/cancelled uchun rollback tugmasi ham bor. |
| 25 | Kuryer: bekor qilingan buyurtmalar tabi (cancelled) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS & Elchi: GET .../courier/orders?status=cancelled — Ikkalasida checkbox bilan tanlab pochtaga yuborish. |
| 26 | Kuryer tab sonlari (badge: waiting/all/cancelled) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: GET /order/courier/orders/counts (getCourierOrdersCounts, 30s refetch) \| Elchi: maxsus counts endpoint yo'q — PCS tab badge'larida real son; Elchi tablarda son ko'rsatilmaydi. |
| 27 | Buyurtmani sotish (sell) — izoh + qo'shimcha xarajat (extraCost) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /order/sell/:id (sellOrder) \| Elchi: POST /orders/sell/:id → order.sell — Elchi multipart/form-data — proof rasm/video yuklash; PCS proof yo'q. PCS extraCost limitini tarifdan client-side hisoblaydi. |
| 28 | Buyurtmani bekor qilish (cancel) — izoh + extraCost | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /order/cancel/:id (cancelOrder) \| Elchi: POST /orders/cancel/:id → order.cancel — Elchi cancel modali qo'shimcha paidAmount (qisman to'langan summa) va proof upload qabul qiladi; PCS-da paidAmount yo'q. |

| 36 | Managerning bir nechta buyurtmani bitta kuryerga bulk biriktirishi | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: POST /orders/assign-to-courier → logistics.order.assign_to_courier (BRANCH/MANAGER/REGISTRATOR), Dispatch sahifasi \| PCS: biriktirish post (jo'natma) domeni orqali (PATCH /post/:id send) — Arxitektura farqi: Elchi order-level assign; PCS post-level. |

| 39 | Buyurtmaga kuryer tarifini tahrirlash (market/courier tarif) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: PATCH /order/:id (updateOrders, tariff modali) \| Elchi: alohida tarif tahrirlash UI yo'q — PCS order-details'da admin/superadmin uchun where_deliver + tarif modali (new/received/on the road). |

| 50 | Filtr panelini yig'ish/yoyish toggle + faol filtr soni badge | ✅ | ❌ | Frontend-only (PCS filter/index.tsx: showFilters state, activeFilterCount badge) \| Elchi: yo'q — PCS filter komponentida mobil/desktop uchun filtrlarni ko'rsatish/yashirish tugmasi va nechta filtr faolligini ko'rsatuvchi pulslanuvchi raqamli badge. Elchi orders sahifalarida clipboard nusxalash umuman yo'q. |

Elchi backend va frontendda hech qanday replacement mexanizmi topilmadi. |

Elchida faqat user detal ichidagi oddiy jadval, alohida statistik endpoint yo'q. |
| 54 | Tashqi buyurtma dublikatini tekshirish (check-duplicate) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST order/check-duplicate {phone, qr_code, integration_id} (REGISTRATOR/ADMIN) \| Elchi: bunday endpoint yo'q — PCS integratsiyalardan kelgan buyurtma takror emasligini tekshiradi. Elchi order-gateway/service'da check-duplicate topilmadi. |
| 55 | Jadval ustunlari bo'yicha client-side saralash (mijoz/narx/sana) | ❌ | ✅ | Frontend-only (Elchi OrdersTable.tsx: sortable columns + sortValue: customer name, total_price, createdAt) \| PCS: order-list'da sort yo'q — Elchi OrdersTable ustun boshini bosib saralash imkonini beradi (3 ustun). PCS order-list jadvalida saralash yo'q. |
| 56 | Filtr/pagination holatini URL query'ga sinxronlash (deep-link) | ❌ | ✅ | Frontend-only (Elchi orders/index.tsx: ORDER_FILTER_KEYS urlParams, filtersKey, history.replace) \| PCS: filtrlar Redux slice'da, URLга bog'lanmagan — Elchida filtr/status/tab URL'da saqlanadi (refresh/ulashishda saqlanadi). PCSda filtrlar faqat Redux state (order-filters slice)da, URL'ga yozilmaydi. |
| 57 | Branch xodimi: qabul qilingan, hali HQga yuborilmagan bekor buyurtmalar tabi | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: GET orders/branch/orders & GET orders/branch/cancelled (BRANCH/MANAGER/REGISTRATOR, holder_type=BRANCH, canceled_post_unassigned) \| PCS: alohida branch tabi yo'q — Elchi filial tizimiga xos: branchda turgan, HQga jo'natilmagan bekor orderlar. PCSda branch ierarxiyasi yo'qligi sababli bunday tab yo'q. |
| 58 | Manager: tanlangan bekor buyurtmalarni HQga yuborish (bulk send-to-HQ) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: orders/index.tsx sendSelectedToHq -> SendToPost.mutate(selectedCancelledIds) (transfer-batch/post oqimi) \| PCS: filial yo'q — Elchi manager bekor tabida checkbox bilan tanlab 'tanlanganlarni HQga yuborish' tugmasi (count bilan). PCSda bu post moduli orqali boshqacha. |

---

## 2. Skanerlash (Scan / QR)

Ikkala loyiha ham QR/barcode skaner orqali buyurtma/pochta/transfer-paketni ochadi, lekin arxitektura tubdan farq qiladi. PCS'da skaner 2 sahifa: ScanPage (faqat kamera) + ScanAndOrder (universal detal-popup) — token `post_` prefiksi yoki to'g'ridan-to'g'ri order-token bo'lishi mumkin, va detal-popup ichida ROL-asosida sell/cancel/partly-sell/rollback/pochtaga-qo'shish/courier-receive/post-confirm amallari bajariladi. Elchi'da esa yagona `GET scan/:token` resolver tokenni prefiks bo'yicha (BTB-/BTR- → transfer-paket, PST- → pochta, aks holda order) ajratib, 3 ta alohida detal-view (order/package/post) chiqaradi; skaner infratuzilmasi boyroq (kamera + klaviatura-wedge skaner + scanner-gate cooldown/duplikat himoya + WebAudio feedback + vibratsiya), market-cancelled-handover QR scan va courier `scan-assign` endpointi qo'shilgan, lekin skaner detalida buyurtma amallari (sell/cancel/partly-sell/rollback/join-post) UMUMAN yo'q — faqat transfer-paketni 'qabul qilish' tugmasi bor.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- ScanPage — kamera QR skaner sahifasi (admin path: scan), barcha rollar (superadmin/admin/courier/registrator/market) Header'dagi skan tugmasidan kiradi
- ScanAndOrder — universal skanerlandan keyingi detal-popup (admin path: scan/:token): order-popup va post-popup ikki ko'rinishda, rol-asosida turli amallar (courier/superadmin/admin/registrator)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- ScanPage (pages/scan/index.tsx) — kamera + klaviatura-wedge QR skaner, ScanLayout ostida, Auth bilan himoyalangan (rol cheklovi route'da yo'q — barcha autentifikatsiyalangan rollar)
- ScanDetailPage (pages/scan/detail.tsx) — token prefiksiga qarab dispatcher: order/package/post
- ScanPackageDetail (ui/ScanPackageDetail.tsx) — transfer-paket detali + 'Qabul qilish' tugmasi + ichidagi buyurtmalar jadvali
- ScanPostDetail (ui/ScanPostDetail.tsx) — pochta detali + buyurtmalar jadvali (faqat ko'rish)
- ScannerCameraModal (shared) — boshqa oqimlarda (batches, dispatch, mails) qayta ishlatiladigan kamera-skan modali

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Kamera QR skanerlash (camera scan, environment-facing) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: @yudiel/react-qr-scanner (klient-tomon dekod) \| Elchi: useCameraQrScanner hook (videoRef + BarcodeDetector/zxing dekod) — Ikkalasida ham faqat front-tomon dekodlash; backend chaqiruvi yo'q. PCS Scanner komponentidan, Elchi o'z hook'idan foydalanadi. |
| 2 | Klaviatura/USB wedge barcode skaner (keyboard scanner) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: useKeyboardScanner hook (keydown buffer + Enter, idleMs=80/clearMs=350) — Elchi USB/Bluetooth wedge skanerni qo'llab-quvvatlaydi (captureEditableTargets bilan input ichida ham); PCS faqat kamera. |
| 3 | Skaner cooldown + duplikat-token himoya (scanner gate) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: useScannerGate (cooldownMs=1800, duplicateCooldownMs=1800) — Elchi ketma-ket/dublikat skanlarni bloklaydi; PCS'da bir martalik scanSuccess flag'i bor (gate yo'q). |
| 4 | Chiroq (torch/flashlight) yoqish-o'chirish | ✅ | ✅ | front-only (camera constraints advanced torch) — PCS torchOn state + advanced:[{torch:true}]; Elchi toggleTorch + hasTorch detektsiya (faqat torch bor bo'lsa tugma ko'rsatiladi). |
| 5 | Muvaffaqiyatli skan audio/vibratsiya feedback | 🟡 | ✅ | front-only — PCS: beep.mp3 + navigator.vibrate(100) faqat success. Elchi: WebAudio sintez bilan success/error/missing 3 xil ohang + har biri uchun alohida vibratsiya pattern. |
| 6 | Skan natijasini token'ga ajratish (URL→token parse) | 🟡 | ✅ | front-only — PCS oddiy split('/').at(-1). Elchi extractScannerToken: /scan/<token> regex + URL parse + decodeURIComponent + fallback validatsiya (boyroq). |
| 7 | Skanerlangan token bo'yicha buyurtma topish (resolve order) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET order/qr-code/:token → orderService.findByQrCode \| Elchi: GET scan/:token → order.find_by_qr msg (prefiks order bo'lganda) — PCS to'g'ridan order endpointiga uradi; Elchi yagona scan resolver orqali. |
| 8 | Skanerlangan token bo'yicha pochta (post) topish | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET post/scan/:id (token `post_` prefiks) → postService.findWithQr \| Elchi: GET scan/:token (PST- prefiks) → logistics.post.find_by_scan — PCS legacy `post_` matn-prefiksi; Elchi strukturali `PST-` token-prefiks. |
| 9 | Skanerlangan token bo'yicha transfer-paket (batch) topish | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: GET scan/:token (BTB-/BTR- prefiks) → branch.transfer_batch.find_by_token (requester roles bilan) — PCS skan oqimida transfer-paket tushunchasi yo'q (faqat order/post). Elchi BTB- (oddiy) va BTR- (qaytarilgan) paketlarni skanlaydi. |
| 10 | Universal skan-resolver (prefiks bo'yicha order/batch/post ajratish) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: ScanGatewayController GET scan/:token — prefiks extractPrefix() bilan branch/logistics/order servisga yo'naltirish + 8s timeout — PCS'da token turi front'da `post_` includes() tekshiruvi bilan ajraladi, yagona backend resolver yo'q. |
| 11 | Skandan keyin avtomatik detal-sahifaga yo'naltirish | ✅ | ✅ | front routing — PCS: navigate(scan/<token>) 500ms delay. Elchi: navigate(/scan/<token>) 180ms + prefetchQuery bilan detalni oldindan yuklash. |
| 12 | Buyurtmani sotish (sell) — skan detalidan | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST order/sell/:id (Roles COURIER) → orderService.sellOrder; extraCost+comment bilan — PCS skan-popup'da 'Sotish' tugmasi + tasdiq. Elchi skan detalida buyurtma sotish amali UMUMAN yo'q (sotish faqat boshqa oqimlarda). |

| 39 | "QR ushbu ro'yxatga mos kelmadi" (missing-in-list) maxsus feedback + ogohlantirish toast | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: onMissing → playMissingOrderFeedback() (alohida 'missing' ovoz/vibratsiya patterni) + api.warning toast (qrNotFound / branchOrderScanMissing) — Elchida 3-xil feedback turi bor: success/error/MISSING. PCS faqat beep (success) bilan cheklangan, 'ro'yxatda yo'q' holati uchun maxsus signal yo'q. |
| 40 | Skan paytida bir vaqtda takror-qabulni bloklash (in-flight dedup guard) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: pendingScanOrderIdsRef (Set) — bir order uchun mutation ketayotgan bo'lsa qayta skan e'tiborga olinmaydi (batchDetail/new_orderDetail) — Tez ketma-ket skanlarda dublikat so'rovlarning oldini oladi. PCS scanAndOrder'da bunday per-id in-flight guard yo'q (scanner gate ham yo'q). |
| 41 | Transfer-paket ichidagi BARCHA buyurtmalarni skanlab/tanlаb ommaviy qabul (receive-orders bulk) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: PATCH transfer-batches/:id/receive-orders → useReceiveTransferBatchOrders (skan bilan bittalab yoki checkbox bilan ko'plab) — PCS'da transfer-batch (BTB/BTR) moduli umuman yo'q. Elchida paket ichidagi orderlarni skanlab yoki tanlab ommaviy qabul qilish alohida funksiya. |
| 42 | Skan ro'yxatida 'Hammasini tanlash' checkbox + qo'lda ommaviy qabul tugmasi (skanga muqobil) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: batchDetail/new_orderDetail — toggleAll/toggleOne checkbox + 'Qabul qilish' tugmasi (receive-orders / orders/receive) — Skanersiz ham aynan shu ro'yxatdan tanlab qabul qilish imkoni. PCS skan oqimida bunday jadval+checkbox+bulk yo'q (bittalab popup). |
| 43 | Audio kontekstni birinchi foydalanuvchi harakatida ochish (autoplay unlock) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: scanShared.ts — pointerdown/keydown/touchstart listenerlari bilan AudioContext.resume() (brauzer autoplay siyosatini chetlab o'tish) — PCS oddiy <audio> beep.mp3 ishlatadi; Web Audio unlock logikasi yo'q. Elchi sintez qilingan ohanglarni ishonchli chiqarish uchun unlock qiladi. |
| 44 | Sintez qilingan ko'p-ohangli skan signallari (Web Audio compressor + 3 pattern) | 🟡 | ✅ | PCS: bitta beep.mp3 fayl \| Elchi: Web Audio API — DynamicsCompressor + success/error/missing uchun ko'p-notali ohang, alohida vibratsiya patternlari — PCS bitta statik ovoz fayli + navigator.vibrate(100). Elchi har holat uchun farqli sintez ohang va farqli vibratsiya raqsi. |
| 45 | Chiroq (torch) qo'llab-quvvatlanishini aniqlash va tugmani shartli ko'rsatish (hasTorch) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: useCameraQrScanner.hasTorch — torch capability bo'lsagina torch tugmasi render qilinadi — PCS torch tugmasini har doim ko'rsatadi (qo'llab-quvvatlanmasa ham), shunchaki constraints'ga torch:true uzatadi. Elchi qurilma imkoniyatini tekshiradi. |
| 46 | Kamera 'mavjud emas' (cameraUnavailable) va 'xato' (cameraError) holatlarini ajratish | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: useCameraQrScanner cameraUnavailable vs cameraError — unavailable bo'lsa 'Qayta urinish' tugmasi yashiriladi/o'chiriladi — PCS yagona 'Kamera yuklanmadi yoki ruxsat berilmadi' xabari va doim Retry. Elchi ikki holatni farqlab UI ni moslaydi. |
| 47 | Token'dan ko'p nomzodli normalizatsiya (URL + path + raw, multi-candidate) | 🟡 | ✅ | PCS: token = scannedValue.split('/').at(-1) (oddiy) \| Elchi: extractScannerToken + normalizeScannerCandidates — /scan/ regex, URL pathname parse, decodeURIComponent, raw fallback (bir nechta nomzod) — PCS faqat oxirgi '/' bo'lakni oladi. Elchi turli QR formatlarini (to'liq URL, faqat token, encoded) ishonchli ajratib, lokal moslashtirish uchun bir nechta nomzod qaytaradi. |
| 48 | Skan natijasini React Query'da prefetch qilib detalga o'tish (cooldown gate bilan) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: queryClient.prefetchQuery(getScanDetailQueryKey) skan muvaffaqiyatida + useScannerGate (cooldownMs/duplicateCooldownMs) bilan — PCS detal sahifasida fetch'ni o'zi qiladi (cache/prefetch yo'q) va scanner-gate (cooldown+duplikat himoya) yo'q. Bu Elchining tezkorlik+takror-himoya jufti. |
| 49 | Klaviatura/USB-wedge skaner editable maydonlarda ham ushlash (captureEditableTargets) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: useKeyboardScanner({ captureEditableTargets: true }) — input/textarea fokusda bo'lsa ham wedge skanni ushlaydi — PCS'da klaviatura/USB-wedge skaner umuman yo'q (faqat kamera). Elchi inputga fokus bo'lganda ham skanni perehvat qiladi. |
| 50 | Paket qabulida xatoni tasniflash (already-received / cancelled / wrong-branch) maxsus matn | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: getPackageReceiveErrorText — backend xabarini parse qilib 'allaqachon qabul', 'bekor qilingan', 'noto'g'ri filial' bo'yicha lokalizatsiyalangan matn ko'rsatadi — PCS umumiy xato toast'i bilan cheklangan. Elchi skan-paket qabul xatosini sabablarga bo'lib tushuntiradi. |
| 51 | Skan-detalda inline natija-banner (success/error) + 2s keyin avtomatik /scan ga qaytish | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: ScanPackageDetail inlineMessage banner + setTimeout(navigate('/scan'), 2000) — PCS navigate(-1) (orqaga) qiladi va inline banner emas, global toast ko'rsatadi. Elchi skanerga avtomatik qaytib keyingi skanga tayyorlaydi. |
| 52 | Skan-token bo'yicha transfer-paketni topish requester (id+roles) bilan (RBAC-aware) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: branch.transfer_batch.find_by_token { token, requester:{id,roles} } (BTB-/BTR- prefiks) — requester filial RBAC'i bilan \| order.transfer_batch.find_by_qr ham mavjud — Scan gateway BTB/BTR tokenni branch-service'ga requester konteksti bilan yuboradi (filial egaligini tekshirish). PCS'da batch tushunchasi yo'q. |
| 53 | Skan-postda qaytarilgan/yangi orderlarni alohida manbalardan normalizatsiya (allOrdersByPostId/orders/items) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: ScanPostDetail.normalizePostDetail — allOrdersByPostId/orders/items dan moslashuvchan o'qish, post_total_price yo'q bo'lsa orderlardan summani hisoblash — PCS post-skan popupida faqat 'sent vs received' holat ko'rsatiladi; ichidagi orderlar jadvali va summani hisoblash mantig'i Elchida boyroq. |
| 54 | Skan-gatewayda har servisga 8s timeout + GatewayTimeout xatosi | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: scan-gateway sendWithTimeout(timeout(8000)) → TimeoutError bo'lsa GatewayTimeoutException('<service> service response timeout') — Mikroservis RPC uchun timeout/timeout-xato himoyasi. PCS monolit bo'lgani uchun bunday gateway-timeout qatlami yo'q. |

---

## 3. Yangi buyurtmalar / Market tasdiqlash (New Orders)

Ikkala loyihada ham 'Yangi buyurtmalar' domeni: yangi (status=new) buyurtmalari bor marketlar ro'yxati -> market ichidagi buyurtmalar -> tanlash/skanlash orqali ularni 'qabul qilish' (PCSda 'pochta'/post yaratish, Elchida 'receive'/mail). PCS markets-only modeli ustiga external+excel+server-skan+check-duplicate+takroriy-telefon ogohlantirish qo'shilgan. Elchi esa markets ro'yxatiga qo'shimcha to'liq FILIAL (branch) kiruvchi-partiyalar oqimini (branches -> batches -> batch detail -> order/batch skanlab qabul) qo'shgan, bu PCSda umuman yo'q. Buyurtma tahrirlash: PCS umumiy order-detail sahifasiga yo'naltiradi, Elchida new_orders ichida maxsus update sahifasi (lock qoidalari, sell/cancel/rollback bilan).

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Yangi buyurtmalar (Sahifa: today-orders/index.tsx) — sarlavha + 'Yangi buyurtmalar mavjud marketlar' tavsifi
- Tab: 'Marketlar' (markets) — superadmin/admin/registrator uchun (tab tugmasi badge bilan)
- Tab: 'Tashqi buyurtmalar' (external) — superadmin/admin/registrator; INTEGRATION domeniga tegishli (bu yerda sanalmaydi)
- Market detali sahifasi (today-orders/pages/orderview/index.tsx) — bitta market ichidagi yangi buyurtmalar ro'yxati + qabul/print/skan
- Rol-spetsifik: market roli -> avtomatik o'z market_id ga redirect (getMarketsByMyNewOrders); operator -> market_id ga redirect
- Rollar: superadmin/admin (narx ko'radi, manage), registrator (print+select+delete-new), market (faqat o'z buyurtmalari, delete-new), operator (faqat ko'rish, select/manage yo'q), logist (markets ro'yxati ko'radi)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Yangi buyurtmalar (Sahifa: new_orders/index.tsx) — HeaderName + Outlet, market roli uchun tablar yashirin
- Tab: 'Marketlar' (markets) — non-market rollar; badge hardcoded '2'
- Tab: 'Tashqi Buyurtmalar' (integrations) — INTEGRATION domeniga tegishli (bu yerda sanalmaydi)
- Tab: 'Filiallar' (branches) — FAQAT admin/superadmin uchun (isAdminRole)
- Market detali (new_orders/components/new_orderDetail.tsx) — market ichidagi yangi orderlar + qabul/print/skan/qo'shish
- Yangi orderni tahrirlash (new_orders/components/new_orderUpdate.tsx) — alohida route /new-orders/:marketId/edit/:id
- Filiallar (new_orders/branches/index.tsx) — jo'natilgan batchlari bor filiallar ro'yxati
- Filial batchlari (new_orders/branches/batches.tsx) — bitta filialning SENT batchlari
- Batch detali (new_orders/branches/batchDetail.tsx) — batch ichidagi orderlar, tanlab/skanlab qabul
- Rollar: superadmin/admin (markets+branches), manager/registrator (markets, qabul transfer-batch orqali), market (faqat o'z buyurtmalari, order qo'sha oladi)

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Yangi buyurtmali marketlar ro'yxati (markets jadvali) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/markets/new-orders (haveNewOrderMarkets) \| Elchi: GET orders/markets/new -> order.find_new_markets_enriched/find_new_markets — Ikkalasida ustunlar: # / Market nomi / Telefon / (narx) / Buyurtmalar soni. Elchi qo'shimcha 'Jami summa' ustuni ko'rsatadi rolga bog'liq emas; PCS narx/jami summani faqat superadmin/admin'ga ko'rsatadi (canSeeTotal). |
| 2 | Market qidirish (markets ro'yxati ustida search, debounce) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/markets/new-orders?search= \| Elchi: GET orders/markets/new?search= — Ikkalasida 500ms debounce. Elchi GlobalSearchInput (Redux) ishlatadi; PCS lokal debounce. |
| 3 | Statistika kartalari (Marketlar soni / Jami buyurtmalar / Umumiy summa) | ✅ | ✅ | Frontend agregatsiya (response ustida reduce) — PCSda 'Umumiy summa' karti faqat canSeeTotal; Elchida 3 karta doim ko'rinadi (Marketlar/Jami orderlar/Jami summa). |
| 4 | Market qatoriga bosib detalga o'tish | ✅ | ✅ | navigatsiya (client-side route) — PCS: navigate(market.id) -> orderview; Elchi: navigate('/new-orders/:marketId'). |
| 5 | Desktop jadval + mobil karta ko'rinishi (responsive) | ✅ | ✅ | — — Ikkalasida mobil uchun alohida card render. Elchi umumiy Table komponenti + mobileRowRender; PCS qo'lda hidden md:block / md:hidden. |
| 6 | Market roli avtomatik o'z buyurtmalariga redirect | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/market/my-new-orders (MARKET,OPERATOR) \| Elchi: navigate('/new-orders/:marketId') -> GET orders/markets/:marketId/new — PCSda operator ham market_id ga redirect bo'ladi; Elchida operator roli ko'rinmaydi (Elchida manager/registrator branch-scoped). |
| 7 | Market ichidagi yangi buyurtmalar ro'yxati (detail) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /order/market/:id (newOrdersByMarketId) \| Elchi: GET orders/markets/:marketId/new -> order.find_new_by_market_enriched — Order kartochkasi: status badge, yetkazish turi (uyga/markazga), mijoz nomi, telefon, manzil, mahsulotlar, narx, sana. |
| 8 | Buyurtmalarni tanlash (checkbox, bittalab) | ✅ | ✅ | client-state — Ikkalasida default barcha orderlar tanlangan holatda yuklanadi. PCS: canSelectOrders (admin/registrator); Elchi: !isMarketRole. |
| 9 | Barchasini tanlash / bekor qilish | ✅ | ✅ | client-state — Header'da 'Barchasini tanlash/bekor qilish' tugmasi + tanlangan soni. |
| 10 | Tanlangan buyurtmalarni qabul qilish (pochta/mail yaratish) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /order/receive {order_ids} (createPost via usePost) \| Elchi: POST orders/receive {order_ids} -> order.receive — Asosiy 'qabul' amali. Ikkalasida tasdiqlash popupi. Hammasi tanlangan bo'lsa -> orqaga qaytadi; qisman -> refetch. |
| 11 | Qabulni tasdiqlash modal (confirm) | ✅ | ✅ | — — PCS: ConfirmPopup '{n} ta buyurtmani qabul qilish'; Elchi: PopupConfirm 'receiveOrderTitle' variant=success. |
| 12 | Manager/registrator qabulni transfer-batch orqali yo'naltirish | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: POST branches/transfer-batches -> branch.transfer_batches.create (shouldUseBranchTransferReceive) — Elchida manager/registrator non-market bo'lsa qabul order.receive emas, branch transfer-batch yaratishga ketadi. PCSda bunday filial-ajratma yo'q. |

PCS: canDeleteNewOrders (status='new' bo'lsa registrator/market ham); Elchi: OrderCard'dagi trash tugmasi (non-market select bilan). |

Birinchi tahlilchi bu funksiyalarni sanagan, lekin owner=elchi_only ekanini belgilamagan. |

Filial domeniga xos, PCSda yo'q. |

Birinchi tahlilchi 'sotish/bekor/rollback'ni umumlashtirgan; partly-sell alohida funksiya. |

Elchida faqat market roli redirect bo'ladi. |

Birinchi tahlilchi tab-switcher mexanizmini alohida funksiya sifatida sanab chiqmagan. |

| 59 | QR-skan arxitekturasi: client-token moslashtirish vs server qidiruv | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: global-scanner backend scan endpointini chaqiradi (receive/scan, qr-code/:token) \| Elchi: useOrderQrScanner lokal yuklangan orderlar token-indeksiga moslaydi, keyin receive mutatsiyasi — Funksional natija bir xil (skanlab qabul), lekin mexanizm farqli — Elchi avval ro'yxatni yuklab token'ni client'da topadi, PCS skanni serverga yuboradi. Partial-partial sifatida ikkala tomonda. |
| 60 | Manager/registrator qabulini transfer-batch endpointiga shartli yo'naltirish | 🟡 | ✅ | Elchi: shouldUseBranchTransferReceive → POST branches/transfer-batches (createTransferBatch) aks holda POST order/receive \| PCS: manager/registrator transfer-batch oqimi mavjud lekin rol-shartli payload almashtirish ko'rinmaydi — Elchi new_orderDetail: manager/registrator rolida receive mutatsiyasi createReceiveOrder o'rniga createTransferBatch'ga almashtiriladi. Birinchi tahlilchi 'transfer-batch orqali yo'naltirish'ni eslagan, lekin bu rolega-bog'liq payload-switch nuansini emas. |

---

## 4. Integratsiyalar / Tashqi buyurtmalar (Integrations / External)

Ikkala loyiha ham tashqi tizimlar bilan integratsiyani qo'llaydi, lekin yondashuv tubdan farq qiladi. PCS to'liq ishlab chiqilgan, prodakshnga tayyor integratsiya markazi: (1) generic 'tashqi saytlar' moduli (CRUD + login/QR-proxy + status-sync queue monitoring + status mapping), VA (2) LDG Cargo uchun maxsus, chuqur ishlab chiqilgan yetkazib-berish-provayderi paneli (sozlama checklisti, virtual kuryer, jo'natma tracking, webhook loglar, reconcile, bulk-redispatch, automation toggle'lar). Elchi backendi PROVAYDER-AGNOSTIK va arxitekturaviy jihatdan kuchliroq (HMAC webhook verify, shipment/receivable/remittance entitilari, generic dispatch_config/field_mapping/status_mapping, idempotent sync-queue + cron), LEKIN frontend yuzasi juda yupqa: faqat integratsiya CRUD ro'yxati + statik detal; LDG-ga o'xshash provayder paneli, webhook-log viewer, shipment dashboard, reconcile/bulk UI umuman yo'q. Ko'p Elchi backend imkoniyatlari (dispatch, search-by-qr, receivables, remittances, retry, sync-queue) faqat 'integrations-ops' demo sahifasiga yoki coverage-hook'ga ulangan, real ishlatiladigan UI'da emas.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Settings → Integratsiyalar (RequireRole admin/superadmin) — IntegrationsRoot 2 ta katta tab
- ├─ 'Tashqi saytlar' (IntegrationsPage): integratsiya kartochkalari ro'yxati, qidiruv, Yangi/Tahrirlash/O'chirish/Test, Sync Monitor modal (Barchasi/Muvaffaqiyatli/Muvaffaqiyatsiz/Kutilmoqda/Eski buyurtmalar tablari), Sync Issues modal
- └─ 'LDG Cargo' (LdgCargoTab) ichki pill-nav: Umumiy holat (Dashboard), Sozlamalar, Jo'natmalar (Shipments), Webhook loglar, Boshqaruv (Control)
- Today-orders → 'external' tab (superadmin/admin/registrator) — tashqi QR skanerlash orqali buyurtma topish/import (external-proxy login + qrorder/find)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- New Orders → 'integrations' (NewOrdersExternalList) — integratsiyalar ro'yxati (table/cards), qidiruv (market_id bo'yicha), status filtri, sana oralig'i, Tahrirlash/O'chirish modal, Yangilash, view-mode toggle
- New Orders → 'integrations/create' (ExternalIntegrationCreate) — platform tanlash + asosiy + ulanish + auth forma
- New Orders → 'integrations/:id' (ExternalIntegrationDetail) — faqat ko'rish: status, synced soni, QR skaner platzholder (statik, hali ishlamaydi)
- integrations-ops (IntegrationsOpsPage) — minimal demo sahifa: receivables jadvali + 'Sync' tugmasi (integration ID input)

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Integratsiyalar ro'yxatini olish (kartochka/jadval + qidiruv) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /external-integration (findAll) \| Elchi: GET /integrations → integration.find_all (pagination/filter/market_id/sana) — PCS kartochka grid + frontend qidiruv; Elchi table/cards toggle + server-side qidiruv (market_id), status va sana oralig'i filtrlari. Elchi'da pagination/meta to'liqroq. |
| 2 | Faol integratsiyalarni olish (registrator uchun, QR skaner manbai) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: GET /external-integration/active (admin/superadmin/registrator) \| Elchi: alohida 'active' endpoint yo'q (faqat status=active filtri find_all'da) — PCS registratorga today-orders external tab uchun faqat faol integratsiyalarni beradi. |
| 3 | ID bo'yicha bitta integratsiyani olish (detal) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /external-integration/:id \| Elchi: GET /integrations/:id → integration.find_by_id — Elchi detail.tsx alohida sahifa (statik scanner placeholder); PCS detal modalda. |

## 5. Pochta / Partiya / Qaytarishlar (Mails / Post / Returns)

Bu domen ikkala loyihada ham "post" (pochta/partiya) tushunchasi atrofida qurilgan: buyurtmalar viloyat bo'yicha pochtaga jamlanadi (order/receive), keyin yo'lga chiqariladi va qabul qilinadi. PCS monolitida pochta logikasi server/src/api/post da, frontend client/src/pages/mails da (superadmin va courier uchun alohida sahifa-daraxtlari, rolga qarab AdminSidebar/CourierSidebar). Elchida pochta REST yuzi logistics-gateway (post/* route'lari) → logistics-service (cmd: logistics.post.*) orqali ishlaydi, frontend src/pages/mails (yagona tabli sahifa, role bo'yicha shartli) + src/pages/returns (transfer-batch direction=RETURN). Asosiy arxitektura farqi: PCSda "pochtani jo'natish" = buyurtmalarni KURYERGA biriktirish (kuryer tanlash modali, super/tashqi kuryer, excel + kuryer cheki), Elchida esa "jo'natish" = pochtani FILIALGA (branch) dispatch qilish; kuryerga biriktirish alohida scan/assign oqimida. PCSda kuryer pochta qabul qilgach "bu buyurtma kelmagan" deb qaytarish so'rovi yubora oladi, admin guruhlangan ko'rinishda tasdiqlaydi/rad etadi — Elchida bu courier-side trigger yo'q, faqat admin approve/reject endpointlari mavjud.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Pochtalar / Bugungi pochtalar (today-mails) — superadmin/admin
- Qaytarish so'rovlari (return-requests) — superadmin/admin
- Rad etilgan pochtalar (refused-mails) — superadmin/admin
- Eski pochtalar (old-mails, region/courier/status/sana filtrli) — superadmin/admin
- Pochta detali (superadmin mail-detail — send+courier assign+reassign+print+delete)
- Rad etilgan pochta detali (superadmin refused-mail-detail — receive canceled)
- Kuryer: Yangi pochtalar (courier/new-mails = on-the-road)
- Kuryer: Rad etilgan pochtalar (courier/refused-mails)
- Kuryer: Eski pochtalar (courier/old-posts)
- Kuryer: Pochta detali (kuryer/mail-detail — scan receive + manual receive + request-return)
- ChooseMail sub-navigatsiya (superadmin/admin va courier uchun ikki xil link to'plami)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Mails — Bugungi (today) tab
- Mails — Qaytarish (return) tab (courier'dan boshqa rollar)
- Mails — Rad etilgan (refused) tab
- Mails — Eski (old) tab (+admin/superadmin uchun 'Barcha partiyalar'/batch_mode dropdown)
- Mail detail (/mails/:id — send-to-branch / receive / receive-canceled / scan-select / print / delete)
- Returns sahifasi (/returns — transfer-batch direction=RETURN, status/sana filtrlar, jadval)
- Manager uchun status-scoped post ko'rinishi (sent/canceled/received) — alohida tab emas, useMails ichida

| 1 | Pochta yaratish / buyurtmalarni jamlash (jamlash — order/receive) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /order/receive (usePost.createPost) \| Elchi: POST /receive → order.receive (order-gateway) — Ikkalasida ham buyurtmalarni qabul qilib viloyat bo'yicha pochtaga jamlaydi. UI bu domen tashqarisida (orders), lekin pochta shu yerda tug'iladi. |

---

## 6. Mahsulotlar (Products / Catalog)

Ikkala loyihada ham Products domeni deyarli bir xil minimal model ustiga qurilgan: product = {name, user_id (market egasi), image_url}. Stock/qoldiq, kategoriya, narx, import/export — IKKALA loyihada ham YO'Q (entity'larda bu maydonlar mavjud emas). PCS monolit: rasm lokal diskka multer diskStorage orqali saqlanadi, va `operator` to'liq rol sifatida mahsulot CRUD'ida ishtirok etadi. Elchi mikroservis: catalog-service RPC handlerlar + api-gateway REST yuzasi; rasm file-service orqali yuklanadi (key qaytadi, public URL gateway tomonidan quriladi), market enrichment identity-service'dan, va search-service'ga indeks sinxronizatsiyasi qo'shilgan. Elchida `operator` faqat branch-dashboard roli — mahsulot tab/CRUD'i yo'q.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Products (Mahsulotlar) — superadmin/admin sidebar (AdminSidebar.tsx)
- Products — market sidebar (MarketSidebar.tsx, faqat o'z mahsulotlari)
- Products — operator sidebar (OperatorSidebar.tsx, market_id orqali biriktirilgan market mahsulotlari)
- Products — registrator sidebar (RegistratorSidebar.tsx)
- Product create sahifasi (/products/create/:marketId) — forma + o'sha market mahsulotlari ro'yxati

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Products — superadmin sidebar (menuConfig.tsx /products)
- Products — admin sidebar (/products)
- Products — market sidebar (/products, faqat o'z mahsulotlari)
- Products — registrator sidebar (/products)
- Create Product sahifasi (/products/create-product/:id) — forma + o'sha market mahsulotlari jadvali
- (Elchida operator/manager/branch/courier rollarida products tab YO'Q)

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Mahsulotlar ro'yxati (jadval, sahifalash) — admin/registrator/superadmin | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /product (findAll) \| Elchi: GET /product -> catalog.product.find_all — Ikkalasida ham server-side pagination (page/limit) + market enrichment (PCS leftJoin user, Elchi identity.market.find_by_ids). |
| 2 | Mahsulot nomi bo'yicha qidiruv (debounced search input) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /product?search= (ILIKE) \| Elchi: GET /product?search= -> find_all (name ILIKE) — PCS lokal 500ms debounce; Elchi GlobalSearchInput (redux search slice, product_search kaliti). |
| 3 | Market bo'yicha filtr (dropdown/SearchableSelect) — admin/superadmin/registrator | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /product?marketId= \| Elchi: GET /product?market_id= (yoki user_id) -> find_all — Ikkalasi ham market ro'yxatini market API'dan oladi; market role'da bu filtr yashirin. |
| 4 | Mahsulot yaratish (name + market) — admin/registrator marketni tanlab | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /product (multipart) \| Elchi: POST /product (multipart) -> catalog.product.create — PCS: SUPERADMIN/ADMIN/REGISTRATOR/MARKET/OPERATOR. Elchi gateway: MARKET/ADMIN/SUPERADMIN (REGISTRATOR create'da YO'Q — farq). |
| 5 | Market o'zining mahsulotini yaratish (market_id = o'zi) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /product (role=MARKET -> market_id=currentUser.id) \| Elchi: POST /product (roles MARKET -> marketId=req.user.sub) — Ikkalasida market_id avtomatik o'ziga set qilinadi. |
| 6 | Operator marketning mahsulotini yaratish (operator->market_id) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST /product (role=OPERATOR -> operatorUser.market_id) \| Elchi: yo'q — Elchida operator mahsulot domenida umuman ishtirok etmaydi (faqat branch-dashboard roli). |
| 7 | Mahsulot rasmini yuklash (drag&drop / file picker, preview) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: multer diskStorage (lokal /home/ubuntu/uploads, image_url=filename) \| Elchi: file.upload RPC -> file-service (key) -> gateway public URL quradi — PCS lokal disk; Elchi alohida file-service (memoryStorage, 10MB limit, MIME whitelist png/jpg/pdf/xlsx). |
| 8 | Rasmsiz yaratishda default rasm qo'yish | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS front: rasm bo'lmasa empty.webp blob'ini yuboradi \| Elchi: rasm ixtiyoriy, default blob yuborilmaydi (image_url null) — PCS frontend default rasmni majburan biriktiradi; Elchi front rasmsiz yuborsa null bo'ladi (jadval render'da Box ikonkasi/placeholder ko'rsatadi). |
| 9 | Yaratishda nom normalizatsiyasi (trim/lowercase/space) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: service normalizeName() (lowercase + single space) \| Elchi: yo'q (name as-is saqlanadi) — PCS duplicate'ni normalizatsiyalangan nom bo'yicha aniqlaydi; Elchi faqat DB unique index (name,user_id) ga tayanadi. |
| 10 | Dublikat nom tekshiruvi (name + market unique) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: service ConflictException (oldindan findOne) \| Elchi: DB unique index 23505 -> RpcException 409 — PCS app-level oldindan tekshiradi; Elchi DB constraint xatosini 409 ga map qiladi (handleDbError). |
| 11 | Market mavjudligini tekshirish (yaratishda) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: userRepo.findOne(role=MARKET) \| Elchi: ensureMarketExists -> identity.market.find_by_id — Elchi cross-service identity chaqiruvi (timeout 5s); PCS lokal repo. |
| 12 | Market 'add_order=false' bo'lsa yaratishni bloklash | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: service add_order===false -> BadRequest; front handleCheck refetch add_order -> permission ekrani \| Elchi: yo'q — PCS marketga 'mahsulot/buyurtma qo'shish' ruxsatini server+front ikkala bosqichda tekshiradi; 'Ruxsat yo'q' ekrani ko'rsatadi. |
| 13 | Inactive market bo'lsa yaratishni bloklash | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi front: getMarketById status -> isInactiveMarketStatus -> submit blok + warning \| PCS: yo'q — Elchi create sahifasida inactive market uchun forma disable + ogohlantirish banner. |
| 14 | Marketning o'z mahsulotlarini ko'rish (My products) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /product/my-products (MARKET,OPERATOR) \| Elchi: GET /product/my-products (MARKET) -> find_all{user_id=sub} — PCS my-products operatorga ham ochiq (market_id orqali); Elchi faqat MARKET. |
| 15 | My products bo'yicha qidiruv/sahifalash | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: GET /product/my-products?search=&page=&limit= (getMyProducts) \| Elchi: my-products parametrsiz (search/page yo'q) — Elchi getMyProducts hech qanday query yubormaydi -> market role'da server-side search/pagination yo'q (front faqat to'liq ro'yxatni oladi). |
| 16 | Market bo'yicha mahsulotlar (create sahifasidagi mavjud ro'yxat) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /product/market/:marketId (findByMarketId) \| Elchi: GET /product/market/:marketId -> find_all{user_id} — Elchi gateway: ADMIN/SUPERADMIN/REGISTRATOR/MANAGER/BRANCH; PCS: ADMIN/SUPERADMIN/REGISTRATOR. |

Elchi har qanday autentifikatsiyalangan user ko'ra oladi -> egalik tekshiruvi farqi. |
| 18 | Mahsulotni tahrirlash (admin/registrator) — Edit modal (name + rasm) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: PATCH /product/:id (update) \| Elchi: PATCH /product/:id -> catalog.product.update — Ikkalasida ham name + ixtiyoriy rasm multipart; Elchi gateway ADMIN/SUPERADMIN/REGISTRATOR, PCS SUPERADMIN/ADMIN/REGISTRATOR. |

PCS operatorga ham ochiq; Elchi faqat MARKET. PCS front esa market editda /product/my/:id ga yuboradi (isMarket flag), Elchi list editi har doim PATCH /product/:id ishlatadi (own route'ni ishlatmaydi). |
| 20 | Tahrirda eski rasmni o'chirib yangisini qo'yish | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS: service eski faylni fs.unlinkSync qiladi \| Elchi: yangi key bilan image_url almashadi (eski fayl file-service'da qoladi) — PCS lokal eski faylni o'chiradi; Elchi yangi URL set qiladi, eski obyekt orphan bo'lib qolishi mumkin. |

Elchi faqat isDeleted=true (name o'zgarmaydi). |

---

## 7. Foydalanuvchilar / Rollar / Identity / Profil / Auth (Users)

Ikkala loyihada ham bu domen "user/identity" yadrosi: rol-asosidagi foydalanuvchi ro'yxati, har rol uchun yaratish formasi, tahrirlash/bloklash/o'chirish, login/refresh/logout, profil. PCS — bitta NestJS monolit (`server/src/api/users/users.controller.ts`, REST `/user/*`); rollar: superadmin, admin, courier, registrator, market, operator, logist. Elchi — mikroservis: REST yuzi `apps/api-gateway/src/{api-gateway,auth-gateway}.controller.ts`, biznes-mantiq `apps/identity-service` (RPC `identity.*`); rollar: superadmin, admin, courier, registrator, market(+market_operator), manager, branch, investor, customer (LOGIST yo'q, o'rniga MANAGER/BRANCH filial modeli). Asosiy farqlar: (1) PCS'da market o'z OPERATORlarini to'liq boshqaradi (yaratish/komissiya/to'lov/statistika/balans) — Elchida operator user CRUD yo'q, faqat finance-service'da earnings/balance/payout o'qiladi; (2) PCS'da Telegram login/link va oylik maosh cron'i bor — Elchida yo'q; (3) Elchida MANAGER/BRANCH filial saga'si, market username + expense-proof + telegram token rotation, my-settings (UI preferences) bor — PCS'da yo'q.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Sidebar 'Foydalanuvchilar' (/all-users) — Admin & Registrator (AdminSidebar/RegistratorSidebar yo'q, faqat Admin)
- Users index (pages/users/index.tsx): rol filtri (superadmin/admin/registrator/market/courier), status filtri (active/inactive), qidiruv, tozalash, 'Yangi foydalanuvchi' tugmasi, statistika kartalari
- All-users-table / Users-table (except-market) / All-markets-table — uchta jadval ko'rinishi
- Create-user dispatcher (5 ta rol tab: admin/registrator/courier/market/logist)
- User-profile (/user-profile/:id) — detal + statistika + edit modal + status switch
- Profile (/profile, overview) — o'z profili: avatar, ism/telefon/parol edit, maosh progress, tarif kartalari
- Roles-permissions (/roles-permissions) — STATIK 'coming soon' rollar ro'yxati (tahrirlash yo'q)
- Login (/login) — telefon+parol
- MarketSidebar: 'Operatorlar' (/market-operators) — operator yaratish/komissiya/o'chirish
- OperatorSidebar: 'Mening buyurtmalarim' (/my-orders), 'Daromadlarim' (/my-earnings)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Sidebar 'Foydalanuvchilar' (/all-users) — admin & manager (menuConfig.tsx)
- UserListPage (pages/users/list): UserStats kartalari, UserFilters (rol: admin/manager/registrator/courier/market/marketing/superadmin; status), qidiruv, 'Foydalanuvchi qo'shish' tugmasi, UserListTable
- UserListTable: status switch (active/inactive), o'chirish (Trash), qatorga bosib detalga o'tish; ustunlar: name/phone/role/status
- CreateUserPage → CreateUserForm (RoleSelector: admin/manager/registrator/courier/marketing — bitta forma, rol bo'yicha maydonlar)
- UserDetailPage (/all-users/:id) → UserDetailWidget: UserStatsCard + 'Tahrirlash' (UpdateUserModal) + market add_order toggle
- Profile (/profile) → bir xil UserDetailWidget (isOwnProfile=true) — updateMyProfile
- identity-ops (/identity-ops) — ELCHIGA XOS diagnostika: adminlar/menejerlar/registratorlar ro'yxati + market'ga buyurtma qo'shish toggle
- LoginForm (/login) — telefon+parol
- BRANCH/MANAGER dashboard sidebarlari (filial roli) — PCS'dagi LOGIST o'rnini bosadi

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Login (telefon+parol) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user/signin (cookie refresh) \| Elchi: POST /auth/login → identity.login (httpOnly refresh cookie + accessToken) — Ikkalasi ham refresh tokenni httpOnly cookie'da saqlaydi; Elchi gateway'da AUTH_THROTTLE (rate-limit) bor, PCS'da yo'q. |
| 2 | Access tokenni refresh qilish | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user/refresh \| Elchi: POST /auth/refresh → identity.refresh (cookie yoki body refreshToken) |
| 3 | Logout (signout) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user/signout (cookie tozalash) \| Elchi: POST /auth/logout → identity.logout (+clearRefreshCookie) |
| 4 | Joriy JWT validatsiyasi (app load'da) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: GET /auth/validate → identity.validate. PCS'da alohida validate endpoint yo'q (profil orqali tekshiradi). |
| 5 | Telegram WebApp orqali login | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST /user/telegram/signin → loginTelegram(initData). Elchida telegram login yo'q (faqat market telegram token notifikatsiya uchun). |
| 6 | Telegram akkauntni joriy userга bog'lash | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST /user/telegram/link (JwtGuard). Elchida ekvivalenti yo'q. |
| 7 | Admin yaratish | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user/admin (SUPERADMIN) \| Elchi: POST /admins → identity.user.create (SUPERADMIN). Maydonlar: name, phone_number, password, salary, payment_day. |
| 8 | Registrator yaratish | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user/registrator (SUPERADMIN/ADMIN) \| Elchi: POST /registrators → identity.registrator.create. Elchida branch_id MAJBURIY + manager faqat HYBRID filialda yarata oladi (PCS'da filial yo'q). |
| 9 | Kuryer yaratish | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS: POST /user/courier (SUPERADMIN/ADMIN) \| Elchi: POST /couriers → identity.courier.create. PCS'da region_id + tariff; Elchida region_id branch'dan keladi (HQ yoki manager filiali), branch.user.assign saga'si bilan. |
| 10 | Super-kuryer (ko'p viloyatga xizmat / barcha viloyatlar) yaratish | ✅ | ❌ | PCS create-courier formasi: is_super_courier, serves_all_regions, regions[] (har viloyat alohida tarif). Elchi create formasида bunday rejim yo'q. |
| 11 | Market (do'kon) yaratish | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: POST /user/market (SUPERADMIN/ADMIN) \| Elchi: POST /markets → identity.market.create. Elchi qo'shimcha: username, expense_proof_conditions; manager faqat HYBRID filialda yarata oladi. Elchi frontда 'marketing' rol tugmasi → market backend. |
| 12 | Logist yaratish | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST /user/logist (SUPERADMIN/ADMIN). Elchida LOGIST roli yo'q — o'rniga MANAGER/BRANCH filial roli. |
| 13 | Manager (filial menejeri) yaratish | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: POST /managers → identity.manager.create (branch_id majburiy, user.save + branch.user.assign saga). PCS'da MANAGER roli umuman yo'q. |
| 14 | Mijoz (customer) yaratish | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS: POST /user/customer (alohida endpoint, ko'p rol) \| Elchi: identity.customer.create FAQAT order yaratish oqimidan (order-gateway ichida), alohida REST yo'q. |
| 15 | Operator (market operatori) yaratish | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST /user/operator (MARKET roli). Elchida operator user CRUD yo'q (operatorlar boshqa kanaldan paydo bo'ladi). |
| 16 | Barcha foydalanuvchilar ro'yxati (filtr: rol/status/qidiruv/sahifalash) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /user?search&status&role&page&limit \| Elchi: GET /users → identity.user.find_all. Elchida manager uchun branch-scope filtri (faqat o'z filiali userlari). |
| 17 | Marketlar ro'yxati | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /user/markets \| Elchi: GET /markets → identity.market.find_all (+ cashbox enrichment FOR_MARKET). |

---

## 8. To’lovlar / Kassa / Balans / Hisob-kitob (Payments / Cashbox / Finance)

PCS — monolit (server/src/api/cash-box + cashbox-history): yagona asosiy kassa + har kuryer/market kassasi, naqd/karta (virtual kartalar tizimi), smena (smena), maosh to'lash, moliyaviy tarozi (analytics/top-impacts), Excel eksport server tomonda. Elchi — mikroservis (finance-service + api-gateway/finance-gateway.controller, frontend FSD entities/payments): xuddi shu yadro (kassa, kuryerdan olish, marketga to'lash, spend/fill, smena, history) + ko'p-filialli model (branch kassasi, branch→HQ remittance, manager settlement, COD FIFO settlement legs courier→branch→HQ→market, operator commission). Elchida YO'Q: virtual kartalar to'liq tizimi, maosh to'lash kassadan (faqat salary-row CRUD), shift current/export, per-user va shift Excel eksport, moliyaviy analytics/top-impacts. PCSda YO'Q: branch kassa, manager settlement, COD FIFO settlement, operator commission financeCoverage.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- To'lovlar (Payments) — index: 3 karta (Berilishi kerak/Asosiy kassa/Olinishi kerak) + filtrlar (operationType, sourceType, createdBy, cashboxType) + tarix jadvali + history popup (superadmin/admin)
- Asosiy kassa (mainDetail) — superadmin/admin: kuryerdan olish, marketga to'lash, sarflash, to'ldirish, maosh to'lash, smena ochish/yopish, Excel eksport, virtual kartalar (CardsManager)
- Kassa detali (cashDetail) — kuryer/market kassasi: to'lov qabul/to'lash formasi, Barcha tarix/Oldi-berdi tab, Excel eksport, smena-required modal
- Virtual karta detali (cardDetail) — karta ledgeri (running balance), kartadan marketga to'lash/sarflash/qo'shish
- Moliyaviy tarix (financial-history / m-balance) — Umumiy ko'rinish/Tarix/Tahlil tablari, charts, top-impacts, source filter
- courier-market-cashDetail (cash-box route) — kuryer/market o'z kassasini ko'radi
- Operator daromadlari (operator-earnings) — market/operator: balans, ishlab topilgan/to'langan, komissiya, daromad/to'lov tarix tablari
- operator-orders — operator buyurtmalar+daromad ko'rinishi
- Sidebar rollari: AdminSidebar/OperatorSidebar/LogistSidebar/RegistratorSidebar/MarketSidebar/Courier alohida

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- To'lovlar (payments/index) — 3 karta (toBeGiven/amountInCashbox/toBeReceived) + 4 filtr (operation_type, source_type, cashbox_type, created_by) + PaymentHistoryTable + given/received PopupSelect (admin/superadmin/manager)
- Asosiy kassa (mainCashbox) — kuryerdan olish, marketga to'lash, spend, fill, maosh (popup console.log-only), Excel (client-side), smena yopish (CloseShiftPopup)
- Kassa detali (cashDetail) — market/courier/branch: to'lov formasi (cash/click/click_to_market), settlement details karti, sana filtri
- Mening kassam (MyCashboxPage) — courier/market/manager o'z kassasi
- Moliyaviy balans (financial-balance) — Overview/History/Analysis tablari (Analysis = 'coming soon')
- Settlement (settlement/index) — COD FIFO: courier→branch, branch→HQ, HQ→market legs + per-order settlement state lookup (debug-style UI)
- Operator hisob-kitobi (finance-operators) — operator balance/earnings/payouts + to'lov qayd qilish (debug-style UI)
- Sidebar: yagona menuConfig.tsx, rol bilan filtrlanadi

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Asosiy kassa umumiy ma'lumot (3 karta: berilishi/kassa/olinishi) + tarix + filtr | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /cashbox/all-info \| Elchi: GET finance/cashbox/all-info → finance.cashbox.all_info — Ikkalasida ham 4 filtr (operationType/sourceType/createdBy/cashboxType) + paginatsiya. Elchida manager uchun settlement-aware (kassadagi_summa/berilishi_kerak). |

Elchida kartalar yo'q — bu butun karta sub-domeni PCS-only (avvalgi ro'yxatda 'both' emas, aniq pcs_only ekani tasdiqlandi). |
| 83 | Kuryerdan to'lov qabul qilishda DESTINATSIYA kartani tanlash (qaysi kartaga tushadi) | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: POST /cashbox/payment/courier (card_id bilan, click to'lov tanlangan kartaga) \| Elchi: karta yo'q — PCS karta tizimi tufayli click to'lovlar aniq virtual kartaga yo'naltiriladi. Elchida bunday yo'q. |

---

## 9. Hududlar / Tumanlar / SATO / Logist / Dispatch (Regions / Logistics)

Ikkala loyihada ham hudud (region) va tuman (district) boshqaruvi + SATO/SOATO kod moslashtirish + dispatch (post/marshrutlash) mavjud, lekin chuqurlik juda farqli. PCS: hudud SEEDLANGAN (create/delete yo'q, faqat boshqarish) va u juda boy "boshqaruv qatlami"ga ega — hududga LOGIST biriktirish (single+bulk), hududga ASOSIY KURYER biriktirish, tumanga nomli kuryer qo'shish/o'chirish, tumanlarni BIRLASHTIRISH (merge), super-kuryer hudud bo'yicha breakdown, kuryerning "Mening viloyatim" tuman statistikasi — bularning HAMMASI Elchida YO'Q (Elchi region.entity'da logist_id/main_courier_id ustunlari ham yo'q, district/logist-assignment sahifalari faqat placeholder-stub). Aksincha, Elchi hudud create/delete'ni qo'llaydi (PCS'da yo'q). Dispatch tomonida ikkalasi ham to'liq Post oqimiga ega (yaratish, jo'natish, qabul, bekor, reassign, return-requests); PCS'da qo'shimcha cancel/all, request-return va old/filter-options endpointlari bor, Elchi dispatch sahifasi esa skaner→kuryerga biriktirish (assign_to_courier) markazli.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Sidebar: Regions/Hududlar (admin, superadmin, logist) — xarita + statistika
- Regions ichki: Districts (faqat superadmin) — drag&drop tuman boshqaruvi + merge + delete
- Regions ichki: SATO management (faqat superadmin) — SATO moslashtirish + tuman side-panel
- Regions ichki: Logist assignment (admin, superadmin) — logistni hududlarga biriktirish
- Region statistics modal — hudud detal stat + tuman kuryerlari add/remove (inline)
- My-region / Mening viloyatim (courier sidebar) — kuryer o'z hududi tumanlari statistikasi
- Courier-bulk (courier sidebar) — skaner orqali ommaviy bekor/sotish (kun yakunlash)
- Post/Send sahifalari (alohida post moduli) — dispatch oqimi

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Sidebar: Regions/Hududlar (admin, superadmin, operator/logist, courier) — SVG xarita + stat
- Region ichki: Districts (superadmin) — FAQAT placeholder stub (sarlavha+tavsif, funksiyasi yo'q)
- Region ichki: SATO management (admin, superadmin) — soddalashgan drag&drop tuman ko'chirish (faqat assigned_region)
- Region ichki: Logist assignment (admin, superadmin) — FAQAT placeholder stub (funksiyasi yo'q)
- Dispatch (Truck) — skaner orqali buyurtmalarni yig'ib kuryerga biriktirish
- Logistics-ops — qaytarish so'rovlari ro'yxati + tasdiqlash (oddiy admin paneli)
- UzbekistanRegionMap — region click → detal modal (tuman+kuryer breakdown, faqat o'qish)

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Hududlar ro'yxati (GET region) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /region (findAll, relations assignedDistricts) \| Elchi: GET /region → logistics.region.find_all — PCS findAll assignedDistricts bilan qaytaradi; ikkalasida ham bor. |
| 2 | Bitta hudud (GET region/:id) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /region/:id \| Elchi: GET /region/:id → logistics.region.find_by_id — Elchi find_by_id districts'ni ham qaytaradi (logisticsApi getDistricts shu orqali ishlaydi). |
| 3 | Hudud yaratish (Create region) | ❌ | ✅ | PCS: YO'Q (hududlar onModuleInit'da seed qilinadi) \| Elchi: POST /region → logistics.region.create — PCS region.controller'da @Post() yo'q — hududlar o'zgarmas. Elchi'da admin/superadmin yangi hudud ochishi mumkin. |
| 4 | Hudud o'chirish (Delete region) | ❌ | ✅ | PCS: YO'Q \| Elchi: DELETE /region/:id → logistics.region.delete — PCS'da hudud o'chirib bo'lmaydi; Elchi'da bor. |
| 5 | Hudud nomini yangilash | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS: PATCH /region/name/:id (UpdateRegionNameDto, audit + actor) \| Elchi: generic PATCH /region/:id → logistics.region.update — PCS'da maxsus name endpoint + activity log + dublikat tekshiruvi. Elchi'da faqat umumiy update DTO orqali (alohida UI yo'q). |
| 6 | Hudud SATO kodini yangilash | ✅ | 🟡 | PCS: PATCH /region/sato/:id (dublikat tekshiruv) \| Elchi: generic PATCH /region/:id → logistics.region.update — PCS'da alohida sato endpoint + unikallik validatsiya; Elchi'da maxsus region sato endpoint yo'q (faqat district sato bor). |
| 7 | Hudud SATO moslashtirish — preview | ✅ | ❌ | PCS: GET /region/sato-match/preview (matched/unmatched/duplicates/missing) \| Elchi: YO'Q (faqat district uchun bor) — PCS SATO-management 'Viloyatlar' tab'i shu orqali ishlaydi. Elchi gateway'da region sato-match endpoint yo'q. |

---

## 10. Dashboard / Analitika (Dashboard / Analytics)

Ikkala loyihada ham bitta rolga-bog'liq "overview" dashboard endpointi mavjud: PCS'da REST `GET /dashboard/overview` (+ /overview/courier, /overview/market, /revenue) bitta NestJS monolit DashboardService → OrderService stats metodlariga; Elchi'da esa api-gateway `GET /analytics/dashboard\|revenue\|kpi\|reports/*` → analytics-service (RMQ `analytics.*`) → order/finance/branch/identity/logistics servislariga fan-out qiladi. KPI yadrosi (qabul/sotilgan/bekor/foyda, top marketlar/kuryerlar reytingi 30 kun ≥30 buyurtma, sotuv-foizi, sana oralig'i filtri, daromad grafigi davr bo'yicha) ikkalasida ham bor. PCS'da boy "AdvancedStatsChart" (davr-bilan-davr taqqoslash kartalari, area/bar/composed toggle) admin uchun; Elchi'da esa qo'shimcha KPI metrikalari (o'rtacha yetkazish vaqti, SLA on-time %, bekor-foizi, kuryer-samaradorligi), kassa-balansi paneli, filial (branch) dashboardi, hudud (region) xaritasi widgeti va backendda order/finance/courier reportlari bor (lekin reportlar frontendga ulanmagan). PCS rolga ko'ra yagona sahifani shartli render qiladi; Elchi alohida DashboardPage/MarketDashboardPage/BranchDashboardPage marshrutlaydi.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Dashboards (yagona /dashboards sahifa, rolga ko'ra shartli render) — superadmin/admin: KPI 4 karta + AdvancedStatsChart + market/courier SalesChart + Top markets/couriers leaderboard
- registrator: KPI 3 karta (foydasiz) + market/courier SalesChart + Top markets/couriers leaderboard (AdvancedStatsChart yo'q)
- logist: KPI 3 karta (foydasiz) + courier SalesChart + Top couriers/markets leaderboard
- market: KPI 4 karta (o'z statistikasi) + Top markets + Top operatorlar leaderboard
- operator: KPI 3 karta (foydasiz) + Top markets + Top operatorlar leaderboard
- courier: KPI 4 karta (o'z statistikasi) + Top couriers leaderboard

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- DashboardPage (admin/superadmin/registrator/branch — '/') — DashboardStatistics (donut+gauge+featured+metrikalar), TopPerformers (markets/couriers), FinancialAnalysis (faqat admin/superadmin), RegionStatsCard (hudud xaritasi); widgetlar Settings'dan yoqiladi
- MarketDashboardPage (market roli — '/') — MarketStatsGrid (qabul/sotilgan/bekor/jarayonda/foyda) + 'Yangi buyurtma qo'shish' + tezkor harakatlar
- BranchDashboardPage ('/branch-dashboard', manager/operator) — bugungi/haftalik/active-batch KPI, Orders/Markets/Packages/Couriers kartalari (manager vs operator visibility farqli)

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Dashboard overview endpointi (rolga-bog'liq KPI yig'indisi) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: GET /dashboard/overview (DashboardService.getOverview → OrderService.getStats/getMarketStats/getCourierStats/getTopMarkets/getTopCouriers) \| Elchi: GET /analytics/dashboard → analytics.dashboard → order.analytics.overview/market_stats/courier_stats/top_markets/top_couriers — Yadro bir xil. Elchi qo'shimcha branchDashboard va registrator uchun profit sanitizatsiyasini qaytaradi. |
| 2 | KPI karta: Jami qabul qilingan buyurtmalar (acceptedCount) | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: overview.orders.acceptedCount \| Elchi: overview.acceptedCount — Ikkalasida created_at oralig'i bo'yicha sanaladi. |
| 3 | KPI karta: Sotilgan/yetkazilgan buyurtmalar (soldAndPaid) + success badge % | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: overview.orders.soldAndPaid + frontda successRate \| Elchi: overview.soldAndPaid + SuccessGauge/FeaturedDeliveredCard — PCS'da StatCard badge'da %; Elchi'da alohida gauge va featured karta. |
| 4 | KPI karta: Bekor qilingan buyurtmalar (cancelled) + cancel-rate % | ✅ | ✅ | PCS: overview.orders.cancelled \| Elchi: overview.cancelled (MetricCard badge=cancelRate) — Elchi cancelRate foizini badge sifatida ko'rsatadi. |

| 6 | KPI karta: Jarayondagi buyurtmalar (in-progress = accepted - sold - cancelled) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: frontda hisoblanadi (DashboardStatistics/MarketStatsGrid) — backend qo'shimcha emas — PCS dashboardida alohida 'jarayonda' kartasi yo'q. |
| 7 | Buyurtma holati donut diagrammasi (sold/in-progress/cancelled) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: OrderStatusDonut (overview ma'lumotidan, qo'shimcha endpoint yo'q) — PCS'da donut yo'q; faqat bar SalesChart va leaderboard bor. |

Elchi tanlovni Redux filter slice'da saqlaydi. |

## 11. Filiallar / Partiyalar / Operatorlar / Investorlar / C2C (Branch system)

Bu domen deyarli butunlay ELCHIga xos. Elchida to'liq filial (branch) tizimi bor: branch-service + branch-gateway orqali filial CRUD, filial turlari (HQ/PICKUP/REGIONAL/HYBRID), egalik modeli (OWNED/PARTNER + per_order_share), filial daraxti, filial xodimlari va filial sozlamalari (config). Shuningdek filiallar aro partiyalar (transfer/return batches: yaratish, jo'natish, qabul, bekor, remaining), investor-service (investor/investment/profit-share) va c2c-service mavjud. PCSda esa FILIAL/PARTIYA/INVESTOR moduli umuman yo'q — uning o'rniga 'market operatorlari' tushunchasi bor (market o'z operatorlarini yaratadi, komissiya beradi, daromad to'laydi). Operator daromad/to'lov qismi ikkala loyihada ham bor, lekin operator CRUD/komissiya boshqaruvi faqat PCSda UI sifatida ochilgan. C2C ikkala tomonda ham amalda ishlamaydi (Elchida 501 stub, REST yuzi yo'q).

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Operatorlar — /market-operators (rol: market) — operator yaratish/o'chirish, komissiya sozlash, statistika
- Operator statistikasi — /market-operators/:id/stats (rol: market) — operator buyurtma statistikasi, balans, to'lov qilish
- Mening daromadlarim — /operator-earnings (rol: operator) — balans/jami/to'langan, daromad va to'lov tarixi tablar
- Mening buyurtmalarim — /operator-orders (rol: operator) — operator o'zi qabul qilgan buyurtmalar ro'yxati
- FILIAL/BRANCH moduli YO'Q — PCSda filial, partiya (transfer batch), investor tushunchasi umuman mavjud emas
- post (Pochta) moduli — kuryer-post tizimi (Elchi 'mails' ekvivalenti), filiallar aro EMAS

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Filiallar — /branches (superadmin, admin) — jadval/karta/daraxt ko'rinishi, yaratish/tahrirlash/o'chirish
- Filial detali — /branches/:id (superadmin, admin) — filial ma'lumotlari, xodimlar, tahrirlash, xodim qo'shish
- Partiyalar — /batches (admin, manager, operator, registrator) — Yangi/Eski/Qaytarish tablari, karta grid
- Partiya detali — /batches/:id — order ro'yxati, QR kod, chop etish, asosiy filialga jo'natish (manager/registrator)
- Filial operatsiyalari — /branch-ops — yangi buyurtmali filiallar, partiya bekor qilish (dev/ops sahifa)
- Investorlar — /investors-ops — investor ro'yxati + qo'shish (dev/ops sahifa)
- Operator hisob-kitobi — /finance-operators — operator balans/daromad/to'lov + to'lov qayd qilish (dev/ops)
- Filial dashboard — /branch-dashboard (manager, operator) — filial bo'yicha statistika
- menuConfig: manager/operator rollari REGIONAL/PICKUP/HYBRID filial turiga qarab turli tablar oladi

| # | Funksiya | PCS | Elchi | Backend / Izoh |
|---|----------|:---:|:---:|----------------|
| 1 | Filial yaratish (Create branch) — tugma + modal forma (name, type, parent, code, phone, region, district, address) | ❌ | ✅ | Elchi: POST /branches -> branch.create \| PCS: yo'q — Elchi BranchFormModal; type=PICKUP/REGIONAL/HYBRID; PICKUP da parent bo'shatiladi. PCSda filial tushunchasi yo'q. |

---

## 12. Bildirishnoma / Bot / Telegram / Realtime / Printer / Log / Sozlama (System & Comms)

Bu domen ikkala loyihada juda boshqacha taqsimlangan. PCS (monolit) da: telegraf-asosidagi 2 ta bot (notify/cancel-bot va order_create-bot), oddiy socket gateway, server-side printer (HTML+PDF), boy frontend "Tizim loglari" sahifasi (activity-log viewer, timeline drawer, tab/filtr), va admin "Sozlamalar" markazi (integratsiya, tumanlar, SATO, rollar, loglar, logist biriktirish). Excel mijoz tomonida (exceljs) qilinadi; alohida file/qidiruv xizmati yo'q. Elchi (mikroservis) da: notification-service ichida raw long-polling bilan 2 bot (notification-bot va order-bot), JWT-autentifikatsiyalangan socket.io realtime gateway (user/role room + chat relay + RMQ ko'prik), to'liq in-app inbox (list/unread/read/delete/dispatch), 9 servis bo'ylab merge qiluvchi audit-log gateway + enrichment, server-side Excel eksport (orders/cashbox/shifts xlsx), alohida file-service (MinIO) va search-service. Lekin Elchi frontend hali bo'sh: notifications/logs/system-ops sahifalari deyarli stub. Sozlamalar Elchida per-user (theme/til/dashboard/profil), PCSda esa admin tizim konfiguratsiyasi — ya'ni ikkalasi bir xil "Settings" nomi ostida butunlay boshqa narsa.

**PCS tab/sahifalar:**
- Telegram bot WebApp login (TelegramBot, /telegram + /authtelegram) — barcha rollar (mini-app)
- Buyurtma yaratish boti WebApp (CreateOrderBot, order-created-bot) — market/operator
- Sozlamalar (SettingsPage parent, /settings) — admin/superadmin: Integratsiyalar, Logist biriktirish, Tumanlar(SA), SATO kodlari(SA), Rollar va ruxsatlar(SA), Tizim jurnallari
- Tizim loglari / Logs (logs-page, /settings/logs) — admin/superadmin: tab(Hammasi/Kirishlar/Buyurtmalar/Kassa/Foydalanuvchilar), qidiruv, sana-oraliq filtr, Timeline drawer
- Moliyaviy tarix / Hozirgi holat (history, /m-balance) — admin/superadmin dashboard
- Send-message (stub sahifa, ishlamaydi)

**Elchi tab/sahifalar:**
- Bildirishnomalar (NotificationsPage, /notifications) — admin: faqat sarlavha, hozircha bo'sh (stub)
- Sozlamalar (SettingsPage, /settings) — barcha rollar: Appearance(theme/til), Dashboard(widget toggle), Interface(sidebar), Profile
- Loglar (LogsPage, /logs) — refresh endpoint test stub (audit viewer EMAS)
- Tizim — analitika va xizmatlar (SystemOpsPage, /system-ops) — Analitika KPI, Health liveness, Token orqali tekshirish
